> DanmuAI is a Windows desktop danmaku assistant tailored for live streamers. It captures full-screen images, invokes vision models (e.g., Doubao, DashScope, MiMo) to generate danmaku in real-time, and displays them as a transparent overlay. Built with FastAPI + pywebview for the web console and PyQt6 for the overlay, it supports multi-monitor, multi-provider, danmaku deduplication, and privacy protection (screenshots never saved to disk).
> <sub>Windows AI danmaku assistant. Web console (FastAPI + pywebview), OBS live overlay (SSE), multi-provider (Doubao, DashScope, MiMo).</sub>

Beyond basic codec functions, it adds geometry-driven spatial intersection queries and efficient 3D line-grid querying for GIS integration, geofencing, spatial indexing, and gridded spatial analysis.

By combining prefix compression with a cache-conscious trie layout, it delivers strong exact-match performance while also supporting prefix range queries, longest-prefix lookup, prefix erasure, and efficient serialization. It is well suited for autocomplete, dictionary indexing, routing, and large-scale string-keyed data stores.
> <sub>C++ implementation of a fast and memory efficient HAT-trie</sub>

> DetSql is a BurpSuite extension built with the Java-based Montoya API to rapidly identify and label HTTP requests that may be vulnerable to SQL injection. It combines multiple detection strategies, including error-based, numeric, string, boolean, and ORDER BY style checks, then correlates response differences using Levenshtein similarity, Jaccard similarity, and response-length thresholds. The result is faster traffic triage with fewer unnecessary interceptions during manual security testing.
> <sub>Burp插件，快速探测可能存在SQL注入的请求并标记，提高测试效率</sub>
